# OMADA_TOOLS.md

Catalogue of every operation in the checked-in official Omada Open API specification, organised by backlog category.

> Generated by `node scripts/generate-tools-catalog.mjs` — do not edit by hand.
> Specification: controller 6.3.0.111 (API 3), fetched 2026-11-18 with `scripts/fetch-openapi.mjs`.

**429/2462 operations have a client route in `src/omadaClient`.** Status is derived statically from route templates; it says a route is called, not that every controller accepts it. Tool names for unimplemented operations are suggestions. The runtime tool inventory is validated separately by `node scripts/check-readme-sync.mjs`.
